📂 FILE: ./flatten_repo.py
==============================
import os
import fnmatch

# --- Configuration ---
output_prefix = "konfio_opentelemetry_full_code_part"
min_chars_per_file = 400_000  # Default limit
max_parts = 10  # Hard limit on number of files

# Extended to include common web/config files
allowed_extensions = {
    ".py",
    ".sh",
    ".md",
    ".yml",
    ".yaml",
    ".txt",
    ".json",
    ".ini",
    ".toml",
    ".js",
    ".ts",
    ".html",
    ".css",
    ".sql",
    "Dockerfile",
    "Makefile",
}

# Base ignored directories (always ignored regardless of gitignore)
base_ignore_dirs = {
    ".git",
    "build",
    "__pycache__",
    "venv",
    "env",
    ".env",
    "node_modules",
    "dist",
    "site-packages",
    ".vscode",
    ".idea",
}
# ---------------------


def load_gitignore(base_path="."):
    """Parses .gitignore and returns a list of patterns to ignore."""
    gitignore_path = os.path.join(base_path, ".gitignore")
    ignore_patterns = []
    if os.path.exists(gitignore_path):
        with open(gitignore_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
            for line in f:
                line = line.strip()
                if line and not line.startswith("#"):
                    # Remove trailing slashes for easier directory matching
                    ignore_patterns.append(line.rstrip("/"))
    return ignore_patterns


def is_ignored(path, ignore_patterns):
    """Checks if a file or directory matches any gitignore pattern."""
    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
        # Match the full relative path or just the base name
        if fnmatch.fnmatch(path, pattern) or fnmatch.fnmatch(
            os.path.basename(path), pattern
        ):
            return True
    return False


def is_text_file(filename):
    return (
        any(filename.endswith(ext) for ext in allowed_extensions)
        or filename in allowed_extensions
    )


def flatten_repo():
    file_list = []
    total_size_estimate = 0

    ignore_patterns = load_gitignore()

    print("🔍 Fast-scanning repository metadata...")

    # --- Step 1: Pre-scan (Optimized: No file reading, uses OS size) ---
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk("."):
        # Filter directories in-place to prevent os.walk from even entering them
        dirs[:] = [
            d
            for d in dirs
            if d not in base_ignore_dirs
            and not d.startswith(".")
            and not is_ignored(os.path.join(root, d), ignore_patterns)
        ]

        for file in files:
            file_path = os.path.join(root, file)
            if is_text_file(file) and not is_ignored(file_path, ignore_patterns):
                try:
                    # Instant size check via OS metadata
                    size = os.path.getsize(file_path)
                    header_len = len(f"\n{'='*30}\n📂 FILE: {file_path}\n{'='*30}\n")
                    total_size_estimate += size + header_len
                    file_list.append(file_path)
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"⚠️ Skipping metadata for {file_path}: {e}")

    # --- Step 2: Calculate dynamic limit ---
    dynamic_limit = max(min_chars_per_file, (total_size_estimate // max_parts) + 1)

    print(f"📊 Total estimated size: ~{total_size_estimate:,} characters/bytes.")
    print(
        f"⚖️ Setting dynamic limit to {dynamic_limit:,} characters to stay under {max_parts} parts."
    )

    # --- Step 3: Write the files ---
    part_num = 1
    current_chars = 0
    # Use errors='replace' to avoid crashing on weirdly encoded text/binaries
    outfile = open(
        f"{output_prefix}_{part_num}.txt", "w", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"
    )

    print(f"📦 Compiling Part {part_num}...")
    for file_path in file_list:
        try:
            with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
                content = f.read()

            chunk = f"\n{'='*30}\n📂 FILE: {file_path}\n{'='*30}\n" + content
            chunk_len = len(chunk)

            # If adding this pushes us over the limit, switch to the next file
            if current_chars + chunk_len > dynamic_limit and part_num < max_parts:
                outfile.close()
                part_num += 1
                outfile = open(
                    f"{output_prefix}_{part_num}.txt",
                    "w",
                    encoding="utf-8",
                    errors="replace",
                )
                current_chars = 0
                print(f"📦 Compiling Part {part_num}...")

            outfile.write(chunk)
            current_chars += chunk_len
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️ Error reading/writing {file_path}: {e}")

    outfile.close()
    print(f"\n🎉 Done! Created {part_num} file(s).")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    flatten_repo()

📂 FILE: ./.gitlab-ci.yml
==============================
# GitLab CI — Builds docs/ via Starlight and deploys to GitLab Pages

stages:
  - build
  - deploy

# Validates the Starlight build on feature branches (no Pages deployment)
build:
  stage: build
  image: node:lts
  script:
    - sh docs-site/scripts/copy-docs.sh
    - cd docs-site && npm ci && npm run build
  rules:
    - if: '$CI_COMMIT_BRANCH != $CI_DEFAULT_BRANCH'

# Builds and deploys to GitLab Pages on the default branch
# --------------------------------------------------
# Pages
# --------------------------------------------------
pages:
  stage: deploy
  image: node:lts
  script:
    - sh docs-site/scripts/copy-docs.sh
    - cd docs-site && npm ci && npm run build
  artifacts:
    paths:
      - public
  rules:
    - if: '$CI_COMMIT_BRANCH == $CI_DEFAULT_BRANCH'

📂 FILE: ./CLAUDE.md

# Kandalf

Agent engineering harness for developing AI-agent projects, specialized in improving conversational AI agents. Kandalf systematically analyses, improves, and validates agent projects through structured discovery, atomic task decomposition, coordinated sub-agents, and quantitative improvement reports.

---

## Python Execution Policy

All Python work in this session MUST use `uv` and project-local virtual environments. System Python and global package installations are never used.

1. **uv availability**: Before any Python operation, confirm `uv` is on PATH by running `which uv`. If `uv` is not found, stop and instruct the user to install it:
   - macOS / Linux: `curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh` (then restart the shell)
   - Cross-platform fallback: `pip install uv`
   Do not proceed with Python work until `uv` is confirmed available.

2. **Virtual environment**: If no `.venv/` directory exists in the current project root, create one before any other Python operation: `uv venv`

3. **Package installation**: Install packages with `uv add <package>` (preferred, updates `pyproject.toml`) or `uv pip install <package>` (venv-scoped). NEVER run `pip install`, `python -m pip install`, or any command that installs to the system or user Python.

4. **Script execution**: Run Python scripts and one-liners with `uv run <script>` or `.venv/bin/python <script>`. NEVER call bare `python` or `python3` — these may resolve to the system interpreter outside the project environment.

5. **Isolation**: Each project maintains its own `.venv/` in its root directory. Never share environments across projects and never modify system Python or global site-packages.

6. **User override**: If the user explicitly requests a specific tool for a single operation (e.g., "use conda for this"), comply for that operation and note the project convention. The override applies only to the explicitly requested operation.

==============================
📂 FILE: ./docs-site/README.md
==============================
# kandalf Docs Site

Astro Starlight documentation site built from `docs/` and deployed to GitLab Pages.

## Local Preview

```sh
# From kandalf/ repo root:
bash docs-site/scripts/copy-docs.sh   # pre-process docs into Starlight content dir
cd docs-site && npm install            # first time only
npm run dev                            # → http://localhost:4321
```

## Build (mirrors CI)

```sh
bash docs-site/scripts/copy-docs.sh
cd docs-site && npm run build
# Output: ../public/
```

## Adding a New Doc Page

1. Create a `.md` file anywhere under `docs/` (with a `title:` frontmatter field)
2. Preview locally with the steps above — the page appears in the sidebar automatically
3. Commit and push — CI deploys on merge to the default branch

## CI

- **Feature branches**: `build` job runs to validate the Starlight build
- **Default branch**: `pages` job runs and deploys to GitLab Pages

==============================
📂 FILE: ./docs-site/scripts/copy-docs.sh
==============================
#!/bin/sh
# copy-docs.sh — Pre-processes docs/ into docs-site/src/content/docs/
#
# Steps:
#   1. Clear destination of .md files (keep .gitkeep)
#   2. Copy .md files from docs/, excluding .obsidian/ and *.canvas
#   3. Rename 00-index.md -> index.md (Starlight landing page)
#   4. Inject title: from first H1 if frontmatter lacks one; prepend frontmatter if absent
#   5. Strip slug: fields that conflict with Starlight URL generation
#   6. Convert Obsidian wikilinks to standard Markdown links with normalised paths:
#      [[path#anchor|label]] -> [label](/slug-path/#slug-anchor)
#      [[path|label]]        -> [label](/slug-path/)
#      [[path]]              -> [last-segment](/slug-path/)
#      [[x.canvas|label]]    -> label  (no link — canvas has no site page)
#      Path normalisation: lowercase, non-alphanumeric chars (except /) → hyphens,
#      consecutive hyphens collapsed. Escaped \| in table cells handled transparently.
#   7. Strip the first # H1 heading from content body (Starlight renders title: as H1;
#      leaving the heading in causes a duplicate title on the page)
#   8. Convert Obsidian callouts (> [!type] Title / > body) to Starlight aside directives
#      (:::type[Title]\nbody\n:::) — runs AFTER step 6 so wikilinks in callout bodies
#      are already standard Markdown links before restructuring
#   9. Tag unlabelled code fences (```) as ```text for consistent terminal-theme styling
#  10. Normalise code fence language identifiers to lowercase (Bash -> bash, YAML -> yaml)
#  11. Inject Starlight hero/splash frontmatter into index.md

set -e

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/../.." && pwd)"
SRC="$REPO_ROOT/docs"
DEST="$SCRIPT_DIR/../src/content/docs"

echo "[copy-docs] Source:      $SRC"
echo "[copy-docs] Destination: $DEST"

# Create destination directory if it does not yet exist (first run on clean checkout)
mkdir -p "$DEST"

# ---------- Step 1: Clear destination .md files --------------------------
find "$DEST" -name "*.md" -delete
find "$DEST" -mindepth 1 -type d | sort -r | while IFS= read -r d; do
  rmdir "$d" 2>/dev/null || true
done

# ---------- Step 2: Copy .md files excluding .obsidian/ and *.canvas -----
find "$SRC" -name "*.md" | grep -v "/.obsidian/" | while IFS= read -r src_file; do
  rel="${src_file#$SRC/}"
  dest_file="$DEST/$rel"
  mkdir -p "$(dirname "$dest_file")"
  cp "$src_file" "$dest_file"
done

# ---------- Step 3: Rename 00-index.md -> index.md -----------------------
if [ -f "$DEST/00-index.md" ]; then
  mv "$DEST/00-index.md" "$DEST/index.md"
fi

# ---------- Steps 4 & 5: Process frontmatter with awk --------------------
process_frontmatter() {
  f="$1"
  h1=$(grep -m1 "^# " "$f" | sed 's/^# //' || true)
  first_line=$(head -1 "$f")

  if [ "$first_line" = "---" ]; then
    # Has frontmatter — inject title if missing, strip slug:
    awk -v h1="$h1" '
      NR == 1 && /^---$/ { in_fm = 1; print; next }
      in_fm && /^---$/ {
        if (!has_title && h1 != "") print "title: \"" h1 "\""
        in_fm = 0; print; next
      }
      in_fm && /^slug:/ { next }
      in_fm && /^title:/ { has_title = 1; print; next }
      { print }
    ' "$f" > "$f.tmp" && mv "$f.tmp" "$f"
  elif [ -n "$h1" ]; then
    # No frontmatter — prepend a minimal one with title
    { printf -- '---\ntitle: "%s"\n---\n' "$h1"; cat "$f"; } > "$f.tmp" && mv "$f.tmp" "$f"
  fi
}

find "$DEST" -name "*.md" | while IFS= read -r f; do
  process_frontmatter "$f"
done

# ---------- Step 6: Convert Obsidian wikilinks to Markdown links ----------
# Path normalisation: lowercase + non-alphanumeric (except /) → hyphens, collapse runs.
# Handles \| escape used in Markdown table cells transparently.
find "$DEST" -name "*.md" | while IFS= read -r f; do
  awk '
    function slugify(s,    out) {
      out = tolower(s)
      gsub(/[^a-z0-9 \/\-]/, "", out)
      gsub(/ /, "-", out)
      return out
    }
    {
      line = $0
      result = ""
      while (match(line, /\[\[[^\]]*\]\]/)) {
        result = result substr(line, 1, RSTART - 1)
        wl     = substr(line, RSTART + 2, RLENGTH - 4)
        line   = substr(line, RSTART + RLENGTH)

        # Normalise escaped pipe from Markdown table cells to plain pipe
        gsub(/\\\|/, "|", wl)

        # Canvas link: emit label only (no URL — canvas pages have no site page)
        if (wl ~ /\.canvas/) {
          n = split(wl, p, "|")
          result = result ((n >= 2) ? p[2] : wl)
          continue
        }

        # Split on | for optional label
        n = split(wl, p, "|")
        if (n >= 2) {
          inner = p[1]
          lbl   = p[2]
        } else {
          inner = wl
          # Bare wikilink: use last path segment as display label (not slugified)
          m = split(inner, segs, "/")
          lbl = segs[m]
        }

        # Handle optional #anchor
        if (split(inner, ap, "#") == 2) {
          result = result "[" lbl "](/" slugify(ap[1]) "/#" slugify(ap[2]) ")"
        } else {
          result = result "[" lbl "](/" slugify(inner) "/)"
        }
      }
      print result line
    }
  ' "$f" > "$f.tmp" && mv "$f.tmp" "$f"
done

# ---------- Step 7: Strip first H1 heading from content body -------------
# Starlight renders title: frontmatter as the page H1 automatically.
# Keeping the # Heading in the content causes a duplicate title on screen.
# Source files in docs/ are NOT touched — Obsidian keeps full H1 headings.
find "$DEST" -name "*.md" | while IFS= read -r f; do
  awk '
    NR == 1 && /^---$/ { in_fm = 1; print; next }
    in_fm && /^---$/ { in_fm = 0; past_fm = 1; print; next }
    in_fm { print; next }
    past_fm && !h1_removed && /^# / { h1_removed = 1; skip_blank = 1; next }
    skip_blank && /^[[:space:]]*$/ { skip_blank = 0; next }
    { skip_blank = 0; print }
  ' "$f" > "$f.tmp" && mv "$f.tmp" "$f"
done

# ---------- Step 8: Convert Obsidian callouts to Starlight aside directives ----------
# Obsidian: > [!type] Title / > body  →  Starlight: :::type[Title]\nbody\n:::
# Runs after step 6 (wikilinks) so links inside callout bodies are already Markdown.
# Type mapping: tip->tip, note->note, warning->caution, important->caution,
#               danger->danger, info->note, (any other)->note
find "$DEST" -name "*.md" | while IFS= read -r f; do
  awk '
    /^```/ {
      in_fence = !in_fence
      if (in_callout) {
        # flush pending callout before toggling fence state
        printf ":::%s[%s]\n%s:::\n", callout_type, callout_title, callout_body
        in_callout = 0; callout_body = ""
      }
      print; next
    }
    in_fence { print; next }
    !in_callout && /^> \[!/ {
      # Parse: > [!type] Optional title
      rest = substr($0, 3)            # strip leading "> "
      gsub(/^\[!/, "", rest)          # remove "[!"
      split(rest, a, "]")             # a[1]=type, a[2]=" title"
      raw_type = tolower(a[1])
      title = a[2]; sub(/^[[:space:]]*/, "", title)
      if      (raw_type == "tip")       callout_type = "tip"
      else if (raw_type == "note")      callout_type = "note"
      else if (raw_type == "warning")   callout_type = "caution"
      else if (raw_type == "important") callout_type = "caution"
      else if (raw_type == "danger")    callout_type = "danger"
      else if (raw_type == "info")      callout_type = "note"
      else                              callout_type = "note"
      callout_title = title
      callout_body = ""
      in_callout = 1
      next
    }
    in_callout && /^>/ {
      # Continuation line: strip "> " or bare ">"
      line = substr($0, 2)
      sub(/^ /, "", line)
      callout_body = callout_body line "\n"
      next
    }
    in_callout {
      # Non-"> " line ends the callout block
      printf ":::%s[%s]\n%s:::\n", callout_type, callout_title, callout_body
      in_callout = 0; callout_body = ""
      print; next
    }
    { print }
    END {
      if (in_callout)
        printf ":::%s[%s]\n%s:::\n", callout_type, callout_title, callout_body
    }
  ' "$f" > "$f.tmp" && mv "$f.tmp" "$f"
done

# ---------- Step 9: Tag unlabelled code fences as ```text ----------------
# Opening fences with no language identifier get `text` for consistent styling.
# Closing fences (inside a block) are emitted unchanged.
find "$DEST" -name "*.md" | while IFS= read -r f; do
  awk '
    /^```[^`]/ { in_fence = 1; print; next }
    /^```$/ {
      if (!in_fence) { print "```text"; in_fence = 1 }
      else           { print; in_fence = 0 }
      next
    }
    { print }
  ' "$f" > "$f.tmp" && mv "$f.tmp" "$f"
done

# ---------- Step 10: Normalise code fence language tags to lowercase ------
# ```Bash -> ```bash, ```YAML -> ```yaml, etc. (POSIX y// transliteration)
find "$DEST" -name "*.md" | while IFS= read -r f; do
  sed -i.bak '/^```[A-Z]/y/ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ/abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz/' "$f"
  rm -f "$f.bak"
done

# ---------- Step 11: Inject Starlight hero/splash frontmatter into index.md ----------
# All values are derived at runtime from index.md and the destination directory
# structure — nothing is hardcoded. Title and description come from the processed
# index.md frontmatter; tagline from the first **bold** line in its body (falling
# back to the first blockquote or paragraph); actions from top-level .md files
# (sorted) followed by the first .md in each subdirectory (sorted by dir name).
INDEX="$DEST/index.md"
if [ -f "$INDEX" ]; then

  # -- Extract title (required) and optional description from frontmatter ----------
  site_title=$(awk '
    /^---$/ { fm++; next }
    fm == 1 && /^title:/ {
      sub(/^title:[[:space:]]*/,""); gsub(/^"|"$/,""); print; exit
    }
    fm >= 2 { exit }
  ' "$INDEX")

  site_desc=$(awk '
    /^---$/ { fm++; next }
    fm == 1 && /^description:/ {
      sub(/^description:[[:space:]]*/,""); gsub(/^"|"$/,""); print; exit
    }
    fm >= 2 { exit }
  ' "$INDEX")
  [ -z "$site_desc" ] && site_desc="$site_title"

  # -- Extract tagline: first **bold** span in body; fall back to first blockquote /
  #    paragraph line. Bold is preferred because it avoids embedded quote issues.
  site_tagline=$(awk '
    /^---$/ { fm++; next }
    fm < 2 || /^[[:space:]]*$/ { next }
    /\*\*[^*]+\*\*/ {
      match($0, /\*\*[^*]+\*\*/)
      print substr($0, RSTART+2, RLENGTH-4); exit
    }
    {
      sub(/^>[[:space:]]*/,"")   # strip blockquote prefix if present
      gsub(/\*\*/,"")            # strip any remaining bold markers
      print; exit
    }
  ' "$INDEX")
  [ -z "$site_tagline" ] && site_tagline="$site_title"

  # Escape embedded double quotes so values are safe inside YAML "..." strings
  site_title=$(printf '%s' "$site_title"     | sed 's/"/\\"/g')
  site_desc=$(printf '%s' "$site_desc"       | sed 's/"/\\"/g')
  site_tagline=$(printf '%s' "$site_tagline" | sed 's/"/\\"/g')

  # -- Collect candidate action files into a temp list -------------------------
  #    1. Top-level .md files (excluding index.md), sorted by name
  #    2. First .md file in each subdirectory, sorted by directory name
  ACTION_FILES=$(mktemp)
  find "$DEST" -maxdepth 1 -name "*.md" ! -name "index.md" | sort >> "$ACTION_FILES"
  find "$DEST" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type d | sort | while IFS= read -r d; do
    find "$d" -maxdepth 1 -name "*.md" | sort | head -1
  done >> "$ACTION_FILES"

  # -- Build the hero YAML block into a temp file ------------------------------
  HERO_TMP=$(mktemp)
  printf -- '---\ntitle: "%s"\ndescription: "%s"\ntemplate: splash\nhero:\n  tagline: "%s"\n  image:\n    file: ../../assets/favicon.png\n  actions:\n' \
    "$site_title" "$site_desc" "$site_tagline" > "$HERO_TMP"

  # One action entry per candidate file; NR drives icon/variant by position:
  #   NR==1 → primary / right-arrow   (entry point)
  #   NR 2-3 → secondary / open-book  (key guides)
  #   NR 4+  → minimal / document     (supplementary)
  awk -v dest="$DEST" '
    NF == 0 { next }
    {
      f = $0
      rel  = substr(f, length(dest) + 2)   # strip "dest/" prefix (no regex on path)
      slug = "/" rel; sub(/\.md$/, "/", slug)

      fm = 0; page_title = ""
      while ((getline line < f) > 0) {
        if (line ~ /^---$/) { fm++; continue }
        if (fm == 1 && line ~ /^title:/) {
          sub(/^title:[[:space:]]*/, "", line)
          gsub(/^"|"$/, "", line)
          page_title = line; break
        }
        if (fm >= 2) break
      }
      close(f)
      if (page_title == "") {
        page_title = rel; sub(/\.md$/, "", page_title); gsub(/.*\//, "", page_title)
      }

      if      (NR == 1) { icon = "right-arrow"; variant = "primary" }
      else if (NR <= 3) { icon = "open-book";   variant = "secondary" }
      else              { icon = "document";     variant = "minimal" }

      printf "    - text: \"%s\"\n      link: %s\n      icon: %s\n      variant: %s\n",
        page_title, slug, icon, variant
    }
  ' "$ACTION_FILES" >> "$HERO_TMP"
  rm -f "$ACTION_FILES"

  printf -- '---\n\n' >> "$HERO_TMP"

  # Append the body of index.md (everything after its existing frontmatter)
  awk '/^---$/{fm++;next} fm<2{next} {print}' "$INDEX" >> "$HERO_TMP"
  mv "$HERO_TMP" "$INDEX"

  echo "[copy-docs] Hero frontmatter injected into index.md"
fi

count=$(find "$DEST" -name "*.md" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
echo "[copy-docs] Done. $count pages ready in $DEST"

import { defineCollection } from "astro:content";
import { docsLoader } from "@astrojs/starlight/loaders";
import { docsSchema } from "@astrojs/starlight/schema";

export const collections = {
  docs: defineCollection({ loader: docsLoader(), schema: docsSchema() }),
};
